MEMORANDUM

TO: Nogma Parish, Auditor General’s Staff
KaG2t Nieto
FROM: Ralph Sharp

SUBJECT: Schedule of Prior Audit Findings

As required by Office of Management and Budget (OMB) Circular A-133, the Summary
Schedule of Prior Audit Findings (SSPAF), which is attached, is prepared to reflect the
status of each prior audit finding reported in the Auditor General’s Report on Audit of the
Federal Financial Assistance Programs of the State of Florida for the fiscal year ended
June 30, 1996 (audit report No. 12983, dated May 19, 1997).

## Page 160

FINDING #1:

RESPONSE #1:

Interest on Advanced Cash:

The College has not established procedures to calculate and remit
interest earned on Federal monies when required by Federal
regulations.

The College has established and implemented procedures to calculate
and remit interest earned on Federal monies when required by Federal
regulations. The procedures are on file in the Budget Office and have
been distributed to the appropriate personnel.

C-20

---

## Page 161

FINDINGS #2:

RESPONSE #2:

Americans with Disabilities Act:

All structural changes in the transition plan had not been completed.
Five items estimated to cost $265,000 were in process and 44 items
estimated to cost $608,949 were without funds for completion.

Under the provisions of the Americans with Disabilities Act, the
College is not required to complete all facility changes identified in the
transition plan by a deadline date. The items are to be addressed as
part of renovation and new construction projects as funds are available.

C-21

---

## Page 162

FINDING #3:

RESPONSE #3:

Vocational Education - Basic Grants to States (CFDA 84.048) Job

Training Partnership Act Program (CFDA 17.250):

The College procedures do not provide for the Financial Aid Office to
coordinate all financial aid given to students so that the students are not
awarded Federal assistance in excess of calculated need.

College procedures have been amended to provide for the Financial Aid
Office to coordinate all financial aid given to students to ensure that
students are not awarded Federal assistance in excess of calculated

need.

Outlined below are three new procedures that will address the finding:

a.

Outside resources that are currently available on the College’s
Accounts Receivable Information Systems (ARIS) are limited
to third party tuition and fees that are credited to the student’s
account. Books and supplies charges are not available on ARIS.

Beginning in January 1997, the interface program from ARIS to
SAM was put into production. Third party outside resources
(THOR) are indicated on each student’s financial aid award
screen and are included in the total amount of financial aid
awarded. Students with zero or negative remaining need are
identified through a report, and their financial aid modified
accordingly.

The bookstores are currently utilizing a new point-of-sale
system. Third Party book charges will be downloaded from this
system and forwarded to the Accounts Receivable Department
and the Computer Center. The SAM system will be updated
and awards will be packaged or repackaged accordingly.

## Page 203

Summary of Schedule of Prior Audit Findings
Tallahassee Community College
For the Fiscal year Ended June 30, 1997
Audit Report 12983

Paragraph No(s): 268/271
Program Area: Student Financial Aid
Brief Description: | Refund Policy

Status: A policy on refunds is now in place in the Financial Aid Office. The policy
includes the Federal mandate to provide pro-rata refunds to student through 60% of the term.
The Financial Aid Office has been doing pro-rata refunds on first-time, first semester students this
year, and have asked the data center to provide information on first-time, first-semester students
who have withdrawn and not followed normal procedures. To ensure proper crediting of the
refund, the Financial Aid Office is utilizing the Federal Refund Form as found in the Handbook on
Financial Aid.

Paragraph No(s): = 272
Program Area: Federal Family Education Loan
Brief Description: | Entrance Counseling

Status: A file is maintained in a FoxPro program that allows us to identify students
who have not participated in pre-loan counseling. Students who have not participated in pre-loan
counseling have their checks held until they participate in one of the pre-loan sessions scheduled
on a weekly basis. The Financial Aid Staff have been reminded to give extra attention to students
loan counseling status. All new students at TCC receive an entrance interview, even if they have
participated in entrance counseling at another institution.

Paragraph No(s) 273/274
Program Area: Federal Family Education Loan
Brief Description: | Exit Counseling

Status: All exit counseling is now performed in the last term of the students
attendance at TCC. The only time an entrance and exit interview is performed simultaneously is
when a student received their first loan check at the end of their last term at TCC. All other
interviews are performed separately. Should a student who indicates that they are leaving the
institution and has simultaneous entrance and exit counseling, return, they will be required to sit
for a second exit interview. Student who do not attend exit counseling shall have the exit

C-63

---

## Page 204

counseling documents mailed to them.

Paragraph No(s): 275

Program/Area: Federal Family Education Loans

Brief Description: | Lender Notification

Status: A program developed by Information Systems now informs the Financial
Aid Office of changes in student status. A copy of the letter mailed to the lender is now kept in
the students file. This report is received several times each month.

Paragraph No(s): 276/278

Program/Area: Federal Family Education Loans

Brief Description: | Over award

Status: Documentation of increases in student budgets is now required prior to a
change in the students file and the awarding of additional funds.

Comment: The student cited in audit had apparently provided documentation, but the
documentation was lost.

Paragraph No(s): = 279/281
Program/Area: Federal Pell Grant Program
Brief Description: | Post-Deadline Increases

Status: The problem with post-deadline Pell Grant increase has been identified as a
problem between TCC and our secondary processor EdTech. Because EdTech does not provide
verification of documents received and transmitted on to the Pell Grant Processor, students who
had problems were identified after the fact. We now require EdTech to verify bathches they
receive. The Financial Aid Office utilizes this information to balance against funds awarded.
